-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+// The task kernels operate on the lower-triangular tiles of a row-major matrix.
+// The two tuned tile sizes keep update operands in private caches while making
+// each task large enough to amortize OpenMP scheduling overhead.
+namespace {
+constexpr size_t kSmallBlockSize = 64;
+constexpr size_t kLargeBlockSize = 96;
+
+bool factorDiagonalBlock(double* const a, const size_t n,
+                         const size_t begin, const size_t end,
+                         size_t& badDiagonal) {
+    for (size_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        const double* const rowJ = a + j * n;
+        double sum = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
+        for (size_t p = begin; p < j; ++p) {
+            sum += rowJ[p] * rowJ[p];
+        }
+
+        const double value = rowJ[j] - sum;
+        if (!(value > 0.0)) {
+            badDiagonal = j;
+            return false;
+        }
+        a[j * n + j] = std::sqrt(value);
+
+        const double inverseDiagonal = 1.0 / a[j * n + j];
+        for (size_t i = j + 1; i < end; ++i) {
+            double* const rowI = a + i * n;
+            double product = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : product)
+            for (size_t p = begin; p < j; ++p) {
+                product += rowI[p] * rowJ[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - product) * inverseDiagonal;
+        }
+    }
+    return true;
+}
+
+void solvePanelBlock(double* const a, const size_t n,
+                     const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd,
+                     const size_t panelBegin, const size_t panelEnd) {
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = a + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = panelBegin; j < panelEnd; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = a + j * n;
+            double product = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : product)
+            for (size_t p = panelBegin; p < j; ++p) {
+                product += rowI[p] * rowJ[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - product) / rowJ[j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void updateTrailingBlock(double* const a, const size_t n,
+                         const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd,
+                         const size_t columnBegin, const size_t columnEnd,
+                         const size_t panelBegin, const size_t panelEnd) {
+    const bool diagonalBlock = rowBegin == columnBegin;
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = a + i * n;
+        const size_t lastColumn = diagonalBlock ? std::min(columnEnd, i + 1)
+                                                : columnEnd;
+        size_t j = columnBegin;
 
+        // Four simultaneous dot products reuse rowI and give the compiler
+        // enough independent accumulators to hide FMA latency.
+        for (; j + 3 < lastColumn; j += 4) {
+            const double* const rowJ0 = a + j * n;
+            const double* const rowJ1 = rowJ0 + n;
+            const double* const rowJ2 = rowJ1 + n;
+            const double* const rowJ3 = rowJ2 + n;
+            double product0 = 0.0;
+            double product1 = 0.0;
+            double product2 = 0.0;
+            double product3 = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : product0, product1, product2, product3)
+            for (size_t p = panelBegin; p < panelEnd; ++p) {
+                const double left = rowI[p];
+                product0 += left * rowJ0[p];
+                product1 += left * rowJ1[p];
+                product2 += left * rowJ2[p];
+                product3 += left * rowJ3[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] -= product0;
+            rowI[j + 1] -= product1;
+            rowI[j + 2] -= product2;
+            rowI[j + 3] -= product3;
+        }
+        for (; j < lastColumn; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = a + j * n;
+            double product = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : product)
+            for (size_t p = panelBegin; p < panelEnd; ++p) {
+                product += rowI[p] * rowJ[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] -= product;
+        }
+    }
+}
+}  // namespace

+    const bool diagonalBlock = rowBegin == columnBegin;
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = a + i * n;
+        const size_t lastColumn = diagonalBlock ? std::min(columnEnd, i + 1)
+                                                : columnEnd;
+        size_t j = columnBegin;
 
+        // Four simultaneous dot products reuse rowI and give the compiler
+        // enough independent accumulators to hide FMA latency.
+        for (; j + 3 < lastColumn; j += 4) {
+            const double* const rowJ0 = a + j * n;
+            const double* const rowJ1 = rowJ0 + n;
+            const double* const rowJ2 = rowJ1 + n;
+            const double* const rowJ3 = rowJ2 + n;
+            double product0 = 0.0;
+            double product1 = 0.0;
+            double product2 = 0.0;
+            double product3 = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : product0, product1, product2, product3)
+            for (size_t p = panelBegin; p < panelEnd; ++p) {
+                const double left = rowI[p];
+                product0 += left * rowJ0[p];
+                product1 += left * rowJ1[p];
+                product2 += left * rowJ2[p];
+                product3 += left * rowJ3[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] -= product0;
+            rowI[j + 1] -= product1;
+            rowI[j + 2] -= product2;
+            rowI[j + 3] -= product3;
+        }
+        for (; j < lastColumn; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = a + j * n;
+            double product = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : product)
+            for (size_t p = panelBegin; p < panelEnd; ++p) {
+                product += rowI[p] * rowJ[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] -= product;
+        }
+    }
+}

    15	// The task kernels operate on the lower-triangular tiles of a row-major matrix.
    16	// The two tuned tile sizes keep update operands in private caches while making
    17	// each task large enough to amortize OpenMP scheduling overhead.
    18	namespace {
    19	constexpr size_t kSmallBlockSize = 64;
    20	constexpr size_t kLargeBlockSize = 96;
    21	
    22	bool factorDiagonalBlock(double* const a, const size_t n,
    23	                         const size_t begin, const size_t end,
    24	                         size_t& badDiagonal) {
    25	    for (size_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
    26	        const double* const rowJ = a + j * n;
    27	        double sum = 0.0;
    28	#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
    29	        for (size_t p = begin; p < j; ++p) {
    30	            sum += rowJ[p] * rowJ[p];
    31	        }
    32	
    33	        const double value = rowJ[j] - sum;
    34	        if (!(value > 0.0)) {
    35	            badDiagonal = j;
    36	            return false;
    37	        }
    38	        a[j * n + j] = std::sqrt(value);
    39	
    40	        const double inverseDiagonal = 1.0 / a[j * n + j];
    41	        for (size_t i = j + 1; i < end; ++i) {
    42	            double* const rowI = a + i * n;
    43	            double product = 0.0;
    44	#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : product)
    45	            for (size_t p = begin; p < j; ++p) {
    46	                product += rowI[p] * rowJ[p];
    47	            }
    48	            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - product) * inverseDiagonal;
    49	        }
    50	    }
    51	    return true;
    52	}
    53	
    54	void solvePanelBlock(double* const a, const size_t n,
    55	                     const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd,
    56	                     const size_t panelBegin, const size_t panelEnd) {
    57	    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
    58	        double* const rowI = a + i * n;
    59	        for (size_t j = panelBegin; j < panelEnd; ++j) {
    60	            const double* const rowJ = a + j * n;
    61	            double product = 0.0;
    62	#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : product)
    63	            for (size_t p = panelBegin; p < j; ++p) {
    64	                product += rowI[p] * rowJ[p];
    65	            }
    66	            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - product) / rowJ[j];
    67	        }
    68	    }
    69	}
    70	
    71	void updateTrailingBlock(double* const a, const size_t n,
    72	                         const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd,
    73	                         const size_t columnBegin, const size_t columnEnd,
    74	                         const size_t panelBegin, const size_t panelEnd) {
    75	    const bool diagonalBlock = rowBegin == columnBegin;
    76	    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
    77	        double* const rowI = a + i * n;
    78	        const size_t lastColumn = diagonalBlock ? std::min(columnEnd, i + 1)
    79	                                                : columnEnd;
    80	        size_t j = columnBegin;
    81	
    82	        // Four simultaneous dot products reuse rowI and give the compiler
    83	        // enough independent accumulators to hide FMA latency.
    84	        for (; j + 3 < lastColumn; j += 4) {
    85	            const double* const rowJ0 = a + j * n;
    86	            const double* const rowJ1 = rowJ0 + n;
    87	            const double* const rowJ2 = rowJ1 + n;
    88	            const double* const rowJ3 = rowJ2 + n;
    89	            double product0 = 0.0;
    90	            double product1 = 0.0;
    91	            double product2 = 0.0;
    92	            double product3 = 0.0;
    93	#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : product0, product1, product2, product3)
    94	            for (size_t p = panelBegin; p < panelEnd; ++p) {
    95	                const double left = rowI[p];
    96	                product0 += left * rowJ0[p];
    97	                product1 += left * rowJ1[p];
    98	                product2 += left * rowJ2[p];
    99	                product3 += left * rowJ3[p];
   100	            }
   101	            rowI[j] -= product0;
   102	            rowI[j + 1] -= product1;
   103	            rowI[j + 2] -= product2;
   104	            rowI[j + 3] -= product3;
   105	        }
   106	        for (; j < lastColumn; ++j) {
   107	            const double* const rowJ = a + j * n;
   108	            double product = 0.0;
   109	#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : product)
   110	            for (size_t p = panelBegin; p < panelEnd; ++p) {
   111	                product += rowI[p] * rowJ[p];
   112	            }
   113	            rowI[j] -= product;
   114	        }
   115	    }
   116	}

   194	                    for (size_t ib = jb; ib < blockCount; ++ib) {
   195	                        const size_t rowBegin = ib * blockSize;
   196	                        const size_t rowEnd = std::min(n, rowBegin + blockSize);
   197	                        const size_t leftPanelToken = ib * blockCount + kb;
   198	                        const size_t destinationToken = ib * blockCount + jb;
   199	
   200	                        if (ib == jb) {
   201	#pragma omp task firstprivate(rowBegin, rowEnd, columnBegin, columnEnd, panelBegin, panelEnd, leftPanelToken, destinationToken) depend(in : token[leftPanelToken]) depend(inout : token[destinationToken])
   202	                            {
   203	                                if (badDiagonal.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) ==
   204	                                    std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()) {
   205	                                    updateTrailingBlock(a, n, rowBegin, rowEnd,
   206	                                                        columnBegin, columnEnd,
   207	                                                        panelBegin, panelEnd);
   208	                                }
   209	                            }
   210	                        } else {
   211	#pragma omp task firstprivate(rowBegin, rowEnd, columnBegin, columnEnd, panelBegin, panelEnd, leftPanelToken, rightPanelToken, destinationToken) depend(in : token[leftPanelToken], token[rightPanelToken]) depend(inout : token[destinationToken])
   212	                            {
   213	                                if (badDiagonal.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) ==
   214	                                    std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()) {
   215	                                    updateTrailingBlock(a, n, rowBegin, rowEnd,
   216	                                                        columnBegin, columnEnd,
   217	                                                        panelBegin, panelEnd);
   218	                                }
   219	                            }
   220	                        }
   221	                    }
   222	                }
   223	            }
   224	#pragma omp taskwait
   225	        }
   226	
   227	        // Only the lower triangle is factorized; preserve the original output
   228	        // contract by clearing the upper triangle in parallel.
   229	#pragma omp for schedule(static)
   230	        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
   231	            std::fill(a + i * n + i + 1, a + (i + 1) * n, 0.0);
   232	        }
   233	    }
   234	
   235	    const size_t bad = badDiagonal.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
   236	    if (bad != std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()) {
   237	        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", bad);
   238	        return false;
   239	    }
   240	    return true;
   241	}
   242	
   243	// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix
   244	void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
   245	    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random
   246	    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite
   247	    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite
   248	    
   249	    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
   250	    unsigned int seed = 42;
   251	    
   252	    // Generate random matrix B
   253	    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
   254	        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
   255	    }
   256	    
   257	    // Compute one triangle of A = B * B^T and mirror it. The seeded input and
   258	    // reduction are deterministic while the expensive O(n^3) work is parallel.
   259	#pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic, 1) proc_bind(close)
   260	    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
   261	        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
   262	            double sum = 0.0;
   263	#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
   264	            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
   265	                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];
   266	            }
   267	            A[i * n + j] = sum;
   268	            A[j * n + i] = sum;
   269	        }
   270	    }
   271	    
   272	    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness
   273	#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) proc_bind(close)
   274	    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
   275	        A[i * n + i] += n;
   276	    }
   277	}
   278	
   279	bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
   280	    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix
   281	    double maxError = 0.0;
   282	    double relError = 0.0;
   283	
   284	    // L*L^T and A are symmetric, so validating one triangle is sufficient.
   285	    // The reduction avoids a second n-by-n temporary matrix.
   286	#pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic, 1) proc_bind(close) reduction(max : maxError, relError)
   287	    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
   288	        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
   289	            double reconstructed = 0.0;
   290	#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : reconstructed)
   291	            for (size_t k = 0; k <= j; ++k) {
   292	                reconstructed += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];
   293	            }
   294	
   295	            const double error = std::fabs(reconstructed - A_orig[i * n + j]);
   296	            maxError = std::max(maxError, error);
   297	            const double rel = error / (std::fabs(A_orig[i * n + j]) + 1e-10);
   298	            relError = std::max(relError, rel);
   299	        }
   300	    }
   301	    
   302	    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", maxError);
   303	    printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", relError);
   304	    
   305	    // Check if error is within tolerance
   306	    if (relError > 1e-6) {
   307	        printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");
   308	        return false;
   309	    }
   310	    
   311	    return true;
   312	}

   315	    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   316	    printf("Options:\n");
   317	    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");
   318	    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   319	    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   320	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   321	}

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+// The task kernels operate on the lower-triangular tiles of a row-major matrix.
+// The two tuned tile sizes keep update operands in private caches while making
+// each task large enough to amortize OpenMP scheduling overhead.
+namespace {
+constexpr size_t kSmallBlockSize = 64;
+constexpr size_t kLargeBlockSize = 96;
+
+bool factorDiagonalBlock(double* const a, const size_t n,
+                         const size_t begin, const size_t end,
+                         size_t& badDiagonal) {
+    for (size_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        const double* const rowJ = a + j * n;
+        double sum = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
+        for (size_t p = begin; p < j; ++p) {
+            sum += rowJ[p] * rowJ[p];
+        }
+
+        const double value = rowJ[j] - sum;
+        if (!(value > 0.0)) {
+            badDiagonal = j;
+            return false;
+        }
+        a[j * n + j] = std::sqrt(value);
+
+        const double inverseDiagonal = 1.0 / a[j * n + j];
+        for (size_t i = j + 1; i < end; ++i) {
+            double* const rowI = a + i * n;
+            double product = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : product)
+            for (size_t p = begin; p < j; ++p) {
+                product += rowI[p] * rowJ[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - product) * inverseDiagonal;
+        }
+    }
+    return true;
+}
+
+void solvePanelBlock(double* const a, const size_t n,
+                     const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd,
+                     const size_t panelBegin, const size_t panelEnd) {
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = a + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = panelBegin; j < panelEnd; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = a + j * n;
+            double product = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : product)
+            for (size_t p = panelBegin; p < j; ++p) {
+                product += rowI[p] * rowJ[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - product) / rowJ[j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void updateTrailingBlock(double* const a, const size_t n,
+                         const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd,
+                         const size_t columnBegin, const size_t columnEnd,
+                         const size_t panelBegin, const size_t panelEnd) {
+    const bool diagonalBlock = rowBegin == columnBegin;
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = a + i * n;
+        const size_t lastColumn = diagonalBlock ? std::min(columnEnd, i + 1)
+                                                : columnEnd;
+        size_t j = columnBegin;
 
+        // Four simultaneous dot products reuse rowI and give the compiler
+        // enough independent accumulators to hide FMA latency.
+        for (; j + 3 < lastColumn; j += 4) {
+            const double* const rowJ0 = a + j * n;
+            const double* const rowJ1 = rowJ0 + n;
+            const double* const rowJ2 = rowJ1 + n;
+            const double* const rowJ3 = rowJ2 + n;
+            double product0 = 0.0;
+            double product1 = 0.0;
+            double product2 = 0.0;
+            double product3 = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : product0, product1, product2, product3)
+            for (size_t p = panelBegin; p < panelEnd; ++p) {
+                const double left = rowI[p];
+                product0 += left * rowJ0[p];
+                product1 += left * rowJ1[p];
+                product2 += left * rowJ2[p];
+                product3 += left * rowJ3[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] -= product0;
+            rowI[j + 1] -= product1;
+            rowI[j + 2] -= product2;
+            rowI[j + 3] -= product3;
+        }
+        for (; j < lastColumn; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = a + j * n;
+            double product = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : product)
+            for (size_t p = panelBegin; p < panelEnd; ++p) {
+                product += rowI[p] * rowJ[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] -= product;
+        }
+    }
+}
+}  // namespace

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+// The task kernels operate on the lower-triangular tiles of a row-major matrix.
+// The two tuned tile sizes keep update operands in private caches while making
+// each task large enough to amortize OpenMP scheduling overhead.
+namespace {
+constexpr size_t kSmallBlockSize = 64;
+constexpr size_t kLargeBlockSize = 96;
+
+bool factorDiagonalBlock(double* const a, const size_t n,
+                         const size_t begin, const size_t end,
+                         size_t& badDiagonal) {
+    for (size_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        const double* const rowJ = a + j * n;
+        double sum = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
+        for (size_t p = begin; p < j; ++p) {
+            sum += rowJ[p] * rowJ[p];
+        }
+
+        const double value = rowJ[j] - sum;
+        if (!(value > 0.0)) {
+            badDiagonal = j;
+            return false;
+        }
+        a[j * n + j] = std::sqrt(value);
+
+        const double inverseDiagonal = 1.0 / a[j * n + j];
+        for (size_t i = j + 1; i < end; ++i) {
+            double* const rowI = a + i * n;
+            double product = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : product)
+            for (size_t p = begin; p < j; ++p) {
+                product += rowI[p] * rowJ[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - product) * inverseDiagonal;
+        }
+    }
+    return true;
+}
+
+void solvePanelBlock(double* const a, const size_t n,
+                     const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd,
+                     const size_t panelBegin, const size_t panelEnd) {
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = a + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = panelBegin; j < panelEnd; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = a + j * n;
+            double product = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : product)
+            for (size_t p = panelBegin; p < j; ++p) {
+                product += rowI[p] * rowJ[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - product) / rowJ[j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void updateTrailingBlock(double* const a, const size_t n,
+                         const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd,
+                         const size_t columnBegin, const size_t columnEnd,
+                         const size_t panelBegin, const size_t panelEnd) {
+    const bool diagonalBlock = rowBegin == columnBegin;
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = a + i * n;
+        const size_t lastColumn = diagonalBlock ? std::min(columnEnd, i + 1)
+                                                : columnEnd;
+        size_t j = columnBegin;
 
+        // Four simultaneous dot products reuse rowI and give the compiler
+        // enough independent accumulators to hide FMA latency.
+        for (; j + 3 < lastColumn; j += 4) {
+            const double* const rowJ0 = a + j * n;
+            const double* const rowJ1 = rowJ0 + n;
+            const double* const rowJ2 = rowJ1 + n;
+            const double* const rowJ3 = rowJ2 + n;
+            double product0 = 0.0;
+            double product1 = 0.0;
+            double product2 = 0.0;
+            double product3 = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : product0, product1, product2, product3)
+            for (size_t p = panelBegin; p < panelEnd; ++p) {
+                const double left = rowI[p];
+                product0 += left * rowJ0[p];
+                product1 += left * rowJ1[p];
+                product2 += left * rowJ2[p];
+                product3 += left * rowJ3[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] -= product0;
+            rowI[j + 1] -= product1;
+            rowI[j + 2] -= product2;
+            rowI[j + 3] -= product3;
+        }
+        for (; j < lastColumn; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = a + j * n;
+            double product = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : product)
+            for (size_t p = panelBegin; p < panelEnd; ++p) {
+                product += rowI[p] * rowJ[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] -= product;
+        }
+    }
+}
+}  // namespace

+bool factorDiagonalBlock(double* const a, const size_t n,
+                         const size_t begin, const size_t end,
+                         size_t& badDiagonal) {
+    for (size_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        const double* const rowJ = a + j * n;
+        double sum = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
+        for (size_t p = begin; p < j; ++p) {
+            sum += rowJ[p] * rowJ[p];

+                product += rowI[p] * rowJ[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - product) * inverseDiagonal;
+        }
+    }
+    return true;

 #include <algorithm>

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+// The task kernels operate on the lower-triangular tiles of a row-major matrix.
+// The two tuned tile sizes keep update operands in private caches while making
+// each task large enough to amortize OpenMP scheduling overhead.
+namespace {
+constexpr size_t kSmallBlockSize = 64;
+constexpr size_t kLargeBlockSize = 96;
+
+bool factorDiagonalBlock(double* const a, const size_t n,
+                         const size_t begin, const size_t end,
+                         size_t& badDiagonal) {
+    for (size_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        const double* const rowJ = a + j * n;
+        double sum = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
+        for (size_t p = begin; p < j; ++p) {
+            sum += rowJ[p] * rowJ[p];
+        }
+
+        const double value = rowJ[j] - sum;
+        if (!(value > 0.0)) {
+            badDiagonal = j;
+            return false;
+        }
+        a[j * n + j] = std::sqrt(value);
+
+        const double inverseDiagonal = 1.0 / a[j * n + j];
+        for (size_t i = j + 1; i < end; ++i) {
+            double* const rowI = a + i * n;
+            double product = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : product)
+            for (size_t p = begin; p < j; ++p) {
+                product += rowI[p] * rowJ[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - product) * inverseDiagonal;
+        }
+    }
+    return true;
+}
+
+void solvePanelBlock(double* const a, const size_t n,
+                     const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd,
+                     const size_t panelBegin, const size_t panelEnd) {
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = a + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = panelBegin; j < panelEnd; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = a + j * n;
+            double product = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : product)
+            for (size_t p = panelBegin; p < j; ++p) {
+                product += rowI[p] * rowJ[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - product) / rowJ[j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void updateTrailingBlock(double* const a, const size_t n,
+                         const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd,
+                         const size_t columnBegin, const size_t columnEnd,
+                         const size_t panelBegin, const size_t panelEnd) {
+    const bool diagonalBlock = rowBegin == columnBegin;
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = a + i * n;
+        const size_t lastColumn = diagonalBlock ? std::min(columnEnd, i + 1)
+                                                : columnEnd;
+        size_t j = columnBegin;
 
+        // Four simultaneous dot products reuse rowI and give the compiler
+        // enough independent accumulators to hide FMA latency.
+        for (; j + 3 < lastColumn; j += 4) {
+            const double* const rowJ0 = a + j * n;
+            const double* const rowJ1 = rowJ0 + n;
+            const double* const rowJ2 = rowJ1 + n;
+            const double* const rowJ3 = rowJ2 + n;
+            double product0 = 0.0;
+            double product1 = 0.0;
+            double product2 = 0.0;
+            double product3 = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : product0, product1, product2, product3)
+            for (size_t p = panelBegin; p < panelEnd; ++p) {
+                const double left = rowI[p];
+                product0 += left * rowJ0[p];
+                product1 += left * rowJ1[p];
+                product2 += left * rowJ2[p];
+                product3 += left * rowJ3[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] -= product0;
+            rowI[j + 1] -= product1;
+            rowI[j + 2] -= product2;
+            rowI[j + 3] -= product3;
+        }
+        for (; j < lastColumn; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = a + j * n;
+            double product = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : product)
+            for (size_t p = panelBegin; p < panelEnd; ++p) {
+                product += rowI[p] * rowJ[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] -= product;
+        }
+    }
+}
+}  // namespace
+
+// In-place blocked Cholesky decomposition. OpenMP dependencies express the
+// factor/solve/update DAG, so updates to separate tiles execute concurrently
+// and completed tiles can immediately feed the next panel (look-ahead).
 bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
-    // A is stored in row-major order
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            
-            if (i == j) {
-                // Diagonal element
-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
-                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
+    if (n == 0) {
+        return true;
+    }
+
+    const size_t blockSize = n <= 1024 ? kSmallBlockSize : kLargeBlockSize;
+    const size_t blockCount = (n + blockSize - 1) / blockSize;
+    std::vector<unsigned char> dependencyTokens(blockCount * blockCount);
+    unsigned char* const token = dependencyTokens.data();
+    static_cast<void>(token);  // Referenced by OpenMP depend clauses.
+    double* const a = A.data();
+    std::atomic<size_t> badDiagonal{std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()};
+
+    // Avoid excessive startup and task-scheduling overhead on small matrices.
+    // Three workers per tile row retains the useful task-DAG concurrency seen
+    // in the update wavefront without starting an unproductive large team.
+    const size_t firstUpdateTasks = blockCount > 1
+                                        ? blockCount * (blockCount - 1) / 2
+                                        : 1;
+    const size_t usefulWorkers = blockCount > 1
+                                     ? std::min(firstUpdateTasks,
+                                                3 * (blockCount - 1))
+                                     : 1;
+    const int teamSize = std::max(1, std::min<int>(
+        omp_get_max_threads(), static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>(
+                                   usefulWorkers,
+                                   static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())))));
+
+#pragma omp parallel num_threads(teamSize) proc_bind(close) shared(a, token, badDiagonal)
+    {
+#pragma omp single
+        {
+            for (size_t kb = 0; kb < blockCount; ++kb) {
+                const size_t panelBegin = kb * blockSize;
+                const size_t panelEnd = std::min(n, panelBegin + blockSize);
+                const size_t diagonalToken = kb * blockCount + kb;
+
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(panelBegin, panelEnd, diagonalToken) depend(inout : token[diagonalToken])
+                {
+                    if (badDiagonal.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) ==
+                        std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()) {

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+// The task kernels operate on the lower-triangular tiles of a row-major matrix.
+// The two tuned tile sizes keep update operands in private caches while making
+// each task large enough to amortize OpenMP scheduling overhead.
+namespace {
+constexpr size_t kSmallBlockSize = 64;
+constexpr size_t kLargeBlockSize = 96;
+
+bool factorDiagonalBlock(double* const a, const size_t n,
+                         const size_t begin, const size_t end,
+                         size_t& badDiagonal) {
+    for (size_t j = begin; j < end; ++j) {
+        const double* const rowJ = a + j * n;
+        double sum = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
+        for (size_t p = begin; p < j; ++p) {
+            sum += rowJ[p] * rowJ[p];
+        }
+
+        const double value = rowJ[j] - sum;
+        if (!(value > 0.0)) {
+            badDiagonal = j;
+            return false;
+        }
+        a[j * n + j] = std::sqrt(value);
+
+        const double inverseDiagonal = 1.0 / a[j * n + j];
+        for (size_t i = j + 1; i < end; ++i) {
+            double* const rowI = a + i * n;
+            double product = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : product)
+            for (size_t p = begin; p < j; ++p) {
+                product += rowI[p] * rowJ[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - product) * inverseDiagonal;
+        }
+    }
+    return true;
+}
+
+void solvePanelBlock(double* const a, const size_t n,
+                     const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd,
+                     const size_t panelBegin, const size_t panelEnd) {
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = a + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = panelBegin; j < panelEnd; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = a + j * n;
+            double product = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : product)
+            for (size_t p = panelBegin; p < j; ++p) {
+                product += rowI[p] * rowJ[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - product) / rowJ[j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void updateTrailingBlock(double* const a, const size_t n,
+                         const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd,
+                         const size_t columnBegin, const size_t columnEnd,
+                         const size_t panelBegin, const size_t panelEnd) {
+    const bool diagonalBlock = rowBegin == columnBegin;
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = a + i * n;
+        const size_t lastColumn = diagonalBlock ? std::min(columnEnd, i + 1)
+                                                : columnEnd;
+        size_t j = columnBegin;
 
+        // Four simultaneous dot products reuse rowI and give the compiler
+        // enough independent accumulators to hide FMA latency.
+        for (; j + 3 < lastColumn; j += 4) {
+            const double* const rowJ0 = a + j * n;
+            const double* const rowJ1 = rowJ0 + n;
+            const double* const rowJ2 = rowJ1 + n;
+            const double* const rowJ3 = rowJ2 + n;
+            double product0 = 0.0;
+            double product1 = 0.0;
+            double product2 = 0.0;
+            double product3 = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : product0, product1, product2, product3)
+            for (size_t p = panelBegin; p < panelEnd; ++p) {
+                const double left = rowI[p];
+                product0 += left * rowJ0[p];
+                product1 += left * rowJ1[p];
+                product2 += left * rowJ2[p];
+                product3 += left * rowJ3[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] -= product0;
+            rowI[j + 1] -= product1;
+            rowI[j + 2] -= product2;
+            rowI[j + 3] -= product3;
+        }
+        for (; j < lastColumn; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = a + j * n;
+            double product = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : product)
+            for (size_t p = panelBegin; p < panelEnd; ++p) {
+                product += rowI[p] * rowJ[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] -= product;
+        }
+    }
+}
+}  // namespace
+
+// In-place blocked Cholesky decomposition. OpenMP dependencies express the
+// factor/solve/update DAG, so updates to separate tiles execute concurrently
+// and completed tiles can immediately feed the next panel (look-ahead).

+    const size_t blockCount = (n + blockSize - 1) / blockSize;
